!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1	"Total RNA was converted to strand-specific Illumina compatible sequencing libraries using the Nugen Universal Plus Total RNA-Seq library kit from Tecan (Mannedorf, Switzerland) as per the manufacturer’s instructions (MO1523 v2) using 12 cycles of PCR amplification for the final libraries. An Anydeplete probe mix targeting both human ribosomal and adult globin transcripts (HBA1, HBA2, HBB, HBD) was used to deplete these transcripts."
